full-spatial-stiffness-20260915T011201_941060Z.json

IDWT's physical premise is one infinite-dimensional manifold with all sectors nested as submanifolds. Particle depth counts macroscopic spatial dimensions; matrix and field-component counts in a calculation add no physical dimensions.

Recorded data and provenance for the associated calculation.

Download recorded JSON

statuspass
checks_total510
scopeFull nonlinear spatial second variation of diagonal polarization and adjacent hopping, and the resulting fixed-positive-population averaged energy Hessian. Negative curvature is not particle inertia or a growth-rate calculation. Other nonlinear field contributions and the normal continuum are not included.
elapsed_seconds197.15469636488706
